### 1. Annotated Coffee Table Mood Board

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-API-and-Prompts/main/images/ui_case130/output.jpg" alt="Annotated Coffee Table Mood Board" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://x.com/Ciri_ai/status/2049721047696732550)

**Prompt:**

```text
Use the uploaded photo as the main base image.

Based on the coffee, drinks, desserts, plates, props, table setting, and overall composition shown in the image, create an aesthetic and emotional result that naturally reflects the real elements in the photo. Do not add any people. Stay true to the actual types of food and drinks, their arrangement, and the atmosphere captured in the original image.

Then, carefully observe each element in the photo and add hand-drawn style annotations that match each subject:

Use white pen-style sketch lines with a rough, slightly uneven, hand-drawn feel

Include object outlines, arrows, and dotted lines to guide visual flow

Add short handwritten Korean notes in a casual, introspective, emotional tone

For drinks: describe taste, temperature, and mood

For desserts: describe texture and flavor

For the space: describe the ambiance

Include a short overall summary of the scene

Subtly incorporate small details like steam, sparkles, hearts, or tiny emotive symbols

Maintain a clean, spacious layout with an Instagram story or magazine memo-like aesthetic

The final result should look like an emotionally styled version of the original photo, without forcing in any elements that don’t naturally belong to it
```

<a id="prompt-03e49c90e7c7d18e8c89"></a>

### 2. Publicación en redes sociales - Retratos de pareja en casa estilo Fujifilm

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971024975_lbj1i7_HHfBSpYXUAARWKh.jpg" alt="Publicación en redes sociales - Retratos de pareja en casa estilo Fujifilm" width="480">

A detailed prompt for generating a 3x3 grid of Japanese home-style couple photos with a Fujifilm film aesthetic.

- **Categorías:** Photography & Portrait
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/zhongying14/status/2051322234082410749)

**Prompt:**

```text
Basado en las fotos reales de dos parejas adultas subidas por el usuario, genera un "{argument name="photo style" default="retrato de pareja en casa estilo japonés Fujifilm"} collage de álbum en cuadrícula de 3x3".

Preserva estrictamente las características de identidad real de las dos personas. Tanto la chica como el chico deben parecerse claramente a las personas originales de las fotos subidas, incluyendo la forma de la cara, proporciones faciales, estructura de ojos/cejas, nariz, labios, línea de la mandíbula, tono de piel, edad, línea del cabello, características del peinado y temperamento general. No los conviertas en rostros occidentales, estilos de estudio coreanos, influencers o desconocidos excesivamente embellecidos. Las 9 fotos pequeñas finales deben mostrar a la misma pareja real, capturadas desde diferentes ángulos, distancias y momentos durante la misma sesión en casa.

## Formato Visual
Genera un collage de 3x3 con 9 fotos pequeñas, con una relación de aspecto general de 1:1. Utiliza líneas divisorias estrechas de color {argument name="border color" default="negro"}, todo colocado sobre un fondo de color {argument name="background color" default="negro"}, como un álbum de fotos casero curado. No es un póster comercial ni un diseño de estudio; evita plantillas repetitivas.

## Estilo Visual Principal
Estilo general: fotografía de estilo de vida japonés, textura de cámara Fujifilm, retrato de pareja real en casa, tomas espontáneas, suave, relajado, tranquilo y narrativo.
Enfócate en los colores y la atmósfera de las películas Fujifilm como Superia, Pro 400H o C200. Las imágenes deben tener una saturación baja sin verse grises, luces naturales suaves, tonos de piel transparentes pero contenidos, ligero grano de película, leve ruido en las sombras y contraste suave. Debe verse como una foto de estilo de vida Fujifilm aireada y con capas.
Evita: textura de iPhone antiguo, compresión móvil, nitidez digital, filtros retro pesados, anuncios comerciales o estilos de comercio electrónico.

## Relación de los Personajes
Ambos son una {argument name="relationship" default="pareja joven china real, íntima y natural"}. Cada cuadro debe mostrar claramente que son una pareja con un sentido de familiaridad y compañía. Las interacciones deben ser realistas y relajadas: inclinarse, hacer contacto visual, mirar hacia abajo y reír, apoyarse en el hombro, leer juntos, pasarse cosas, preparar comida, ajustar la ropa, verse en el espejo o mirar desde una puerta. Íntimo pero contenido: sin cursilería, sin erotismo y sin actuación al estilo de una boda posada.

## Expresiones y Ángulos
Las 9 fotos no deben mirar todas a la cámara ni solo sonreír levemente. Las expresiones deben ser ricas y variadas: risitas, ser objeto de bromas, mirar hacia abajo riendo, tranquilidad, soñar despierto, mirada suave, charla relajada, concentrados en una tarea, responder con una mirada hacia atrás, cerrar los ojos riendo, mirar por la ventana o mirarse el uno al otro.
Los ángulos faciales deben variar significativamente. No repitas la misma plantilla. Usa una mezcla de: frente, semi-frente, perfil, mirando hacia abajo, mirando hacia atrás, mirando ligeramente hacia arriba, casi de espaldas, ángulos de espejo, observación a distancia y detalles de vida en primer plano. Cada cuadro debe tener una nueva dirección de cabeza y ritmo visual.

## Requisitos de Vestimenta
No utilices la ropa original de las fotos subidas. Cámbiala por atuendos de estilo de vida japonés natural: baja saturación, colores neutros, materiales suaves. Colores como blanco crema, avena, gris claro, café pálido, azul brumoso o carbón. Las prendas incluyen cárdigans de punto, camisetas suaves, camisas de lino, ropa de estar por casa, faldas largas o calcetines. Debe parecer ropa cómoda que una pareja real usaría en casa.

## Entorno Doméstico
Un apartamento limpio y ordenado con rastros de vida real (no una casa modelo). Incluye elementos como cortinas, pisos de madera, sofás, almohadas, mantas, libros, álbumes, tazas, platos, plantas, lámparas de noche, espejos o mesas; todo como ayudas sutiles para enfocarse en la pareja.

## Contenido de los 9 Cuadros
1. Tranquilidad junto a la ventana: El chico mira hacia afuera; la chica se inclina naturalmente. Atmósfera tranquila.
2. Leyendo en el sofá: Leyendo un libro juntos; uno se apoya en el hombro del otro.
3. Pasando un plato: En la mesa del comedor, pasando fruta; movimiento activo y contacto visual.
4. Preparación en la cocina: Preparando comida; uno corta fruta mientras el otro observa y ríe.
5. Vista desde la puerta: Perspectiva del observador a través del marco de una puerta hacia la sala de estar.
6. Contacto en el dormitorio: Sentados en la cama; la chica ajusta el cuello del chico suavemente.
7. Fotos en el suelo: Sentados en la alfombra mirando revistas juntos; poses sentadas espontáneas.
8. Fragmento de espejo: Capturado a través de un espejo de tocador; ajustando el cabello o una mirada breve en el espejo.
9. Pausa en el balcón: De pie junto a la puerta corredera; uno mira hacia afuera mientras el otro se acerca con una sonrisa.

## Consistencia y Estabilidad
Mantén la consistencia facial en los 9 cuadros. Evita gestos complejos con las manos (nada de tocarse la cara o cruzar los dedos). La calidad debe incluir un ligero grano de película y textura de escaneo para que se sienta como un conjunto real, no una cuadrícula de IA perfecta. Evita artefactos digitales, extremidades adicionales o texto extraño.
```

### 3. Publicación en redes sociales - Diario de stickers con clones chibi

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971003063_000rup_HHf9C1UWMAAOPD4.jpg" alt="Publicación en redes sociales - Diario de stickers con clones chibi" width="480">

A creative prompt that transforms a real photo into a scrapbook diary style with multiple chibi clones of the subject.

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/miilesus/status/2051386554015449281)

**Prompt:**

```text
Crea una imagen de alta calidad de tipo “{argument name="style" default="chibi clone sticker diary photo"}” basada en la foto real subida. Preserva la identidad, el rostro, el peinado, el color de cabello, el atuendo, las proporciones corporales, la pose, la iluminación y el fondo de la persona original. No alteres los rasgos faciales ni conviertas al sujeto en una ilustración completa; mantén un aspecto de fotografía realista.
Analiza la escena como un momento de estilo de vida de {argument name="activity" default="fitness/running"}. Añade de 5 a 8 mini clones chibi de la misma persona alrededor del sujeto, diseñados con un estilo de sticker kawaii consistente (cabeza grande, cuerpo pequeño, ojos grandes y expresivos, acabado digital limpio). Cada clon debe parecerse claramente a la persona real (mismo cabello, atuendo y colores).
Diseña cada chibi con diferentes {argument name="action theme" default="running-related actions"} y emociones: trotando, estirando, bebiendo agua, sintiéndose cansado, animando, dando pulgares arriba, celebrando la meta. Asegúrate de que todas las poses sean únicas y contextualmente relevantes.
Renderiza cada chibi como un sticker con bordes blancos, sombras suaves y un efecto de ligera flotación. Distribúyelos alrededor del sujeto y en los bordes sin cubrir el rostro ni el cuerpo principal.
Añade garabatos hechos a mano (corazones, destellos, flechas, líneas de movimiento, círculos) en color blanco con sutiles acentos rosados, manteniendo una estética limpia de diario de recortes.
Incluye de 5 a 8 frases cortas con estilo de escritura a mano que coincidan con un estado de ánimo fitness (lindo, energético, alentador). Usa principalmente texto blanco con ligeros toques rosados y pequeñas marcas decorativas.
Composición: mantén a la persona real como el enfoque central, rodeada por los stickers chibi y los garabatos. El resultado debe sentirse como una imagen de diario de estilo de vida para redes sociales, pulida, divertida y de alta resolución; limpia, equilibrada y visualmente rica sin verse saturada.
```

### 5. Cómic / Guion gráfico - Hoja de estudio de salto: Squash and Stretch

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971046177_dbw6gh_HHeUIYyXYAA9plV.jpg" alt="Cómic / Guion gráfico - Hoja de estudio de salto: Squash and Stretch" width="480">

Generates a seven-stage animation movement sheet from a character reference for studying jump timing, squash, stretch, and recovery poses.

- **Categorías:** Comic & Story
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/techhalla/status/2051273934356595196#reversed-1)

**Prompt:**

```text
Usando REFERENCE_0 como base del personaje, crea una hoja de movimiento de animación limpia que convierta al personaje en un estudio de secuencia de salto de cuerpo completo, preservando el mismo atuendo, insignia, cabeza calva, zapatos, estilo de ilustración de tinta y color, y el tratamiento de rostro censurado/difuminado. Amplía la vista del retrato recortado a un diseño amplio con textura de papel beige que incluya una línea de suelo, líneas de movimiento sutiles, pequeñas marcas de impacto y tipografía instructiva. Agrega el título {argument name="title text" default="SQUASH AND STRETCH"} en la parte superior izquierda con el subtítulo {argument name="subtitle text" default="JUMP SEQUENCE — 7 STAGES"}. Muestra exactamente 7 poses numeradas dispuestas de izquierda a derecha a lo largo de la página: 1 “ANTICIPATION (CROUCHING)” con el personaje agachado y los brazos hacia atrás; 2 “EXTREME SQUASH (COMPRESSED)” con un agachado más compacto; 3 “EXTREME STRETCH (TAKEOFF)” con el personaje alargado hacia arriba, brazos levantados y pies despegándose del suelo; 4 “APEX (MID-AIR ARC)” con el personaje encogido en el aire; 5 “STRETCH (DOWNWARD ACCELERATION)” con el personaje estirado verticalmente mientras desciende; 6 “SQUASH (IMPACT LANDING)” con el personaje comprimido al aterrizar, con una mano tocando el suelo; 7 “RECOVERY (RETURN TO STANCE)” con el personaje de pie y relajado con las manos en los bolsillos. Usa etiquetas en negrita, mayúsculas y color negro debajo de cada etapa numerada, mantén proporciones consistentes del personaje en todas las poses, excepto por la deformación intencional de squash y stretch, y haz que la hoja se lea como una tabla de referencia profesional de animación 2D.
```

### 7. Cómic / Guion gráfico - Duelo Cyberpunk: Ángel vs. Demonio

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971002323_juaabg_HHfXvHobkAA71yL.jpg" alt="Cómic / Guion gráfico - Duelo Cyberpunk: Ángel vs. Demonio" width="480">

A highly detailed prompt for a futuristic city rooftop scene featuring an angel-styled model and a menacing demon bunny.

- **Categorías:** Comic & Story
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/YaZoraiz/status/2051345377740443844)

**Prompt:**

```text
Duelo cinematográfico hiperrealista en una azotea durante la noche en una megaciudad futurista. Un modelo con estilo urbano vistiendo una {argument name="clothing" default="sudadera blanca oversize con alas de ángel emplumadas detalladas"} se encuentra de un lado, brillando suavemente con una luz de contorno divina y una presencia tranquila y poderosa. Frente a él hay un {argument name="opponent" default="conejo demonio de caricatura estilizado, pequeño pero amenazante"} que viste una {argument name="opponent outfit" default="chaqueta de cuero roja"}, cuernos rojos brillantes y ojos iluminados con neón, irradiando una energía caótica.

Detrás de ellos se extiende un vasto horizonte de ciudad cyberpunk lleno de vallas publicitarias holográficas y ventanas brillantes. Arriba, una tormenta divide el cielo en dos: una mitad iluminada con una suave luz celestial y nubes a la deriva, la otra consumida por truenos oscuros, con relámpagos rojos eléctricos desgarrando la atmósfera.

Iluminación de contraste dramático, texturas ultra detalladas, profundidad de campo reducida, encuadre cinematográfico, realismo 8K, tono de fantasía surrealista, composición de enfrentamiento intenso, movimiento del viento en la ropa y las alas, atmósfera cargada de tensión y simbolismo.
```

### 10. Publicación en redes sociales - Foto callejera de Akihabara en los años 90

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971070679_ltevk4_HHeu6VDbAAAijQh.jpg" alt="Publicación en redes sociales - Foto callejera de Akihabara en los años 90" width="480">

Generates a realistic VHS-style 1990s Akihabara electronics district street scene for retro documentary or historical urban imagery.

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/pipipimayomayo/status/2051300477275660460#reversed-0)

**Prompt:**

```text
Crea una fotografía documental fotorrealista de {argument name="location and era" default="Akihabara, Tokio en la década de 1990"}, tomada a la altura de los ojos en una estrecha calle comercial de electrónica al atardecer o a última hora de la tarde. La escena contiene alrededor de 30 peatones visibles, en su mayoría hombres adultos con ropa casual de los 90, como chaquetas de mezclilla, suéteres de cuadros, jeans de lavado claro, chaquetas tipo bomber, trajes de negocios, bolsos de hombro y bolsas de compras; los rostros son suaves, indistintos o con desenfoque de movimiento, como una vieja captura de pantalla de televisión. La calle está llena pero no caótica, con personas de pie, mirando escaparates, caminando lejos de la cámara y reunidas cerca de las tiendas. Ambos lados de la calle están bordeados por altos edificios comerciales cubiertos de densa señalización de tiendas de electrónica japonesas, incluyendo grandes letreros verticales y horizontales con fragmentos legibles como {argument name="main sign text" default="オノデン"}, {argument name="store brand text" default="Panasonic"}, “FAN”, “BEST” y otros letreros de tiendas en katakana. El lado izquierdo tiene un prominente toldo azul y amarillo de una tienda de electrónica, mientras que el lado derecho tiene un toldo naranja y azul, estantes, carteles y mercancía en cajas exhibida en la entrada. Los cables de servicios públicos se cruzan sobre el cielo brillante y deslavado entre los edificios de mediana altura. Utiliza una estética realista de videocámara de consumo japonesa de los 90 o de transmisión VHS: relación de aspecto 4:3, resolución ligeramente baja, suavidad entrelazada, colores apagados, grano de película suave, luces altas quemadas en el cielo, sutil distorsión de barril, desorden natural de la multitud y sin teléfonos inteligentes modernos, vallas publicitarias LED, moda contemporánea o elementos futuristas.
```

A prompt to edit a gym photo by adding multiple chibi versions of the subject performing various workout activities.

**Prompt:**

```text
Edita la imagen manteniendo la foto original completamente intacta, incluyendo a la persona, el rostro, el cuerpo, la pose, la iluminación y el fondo del gimnasio. Añade múltiples {argument name="sticker style" default="“mini versiones” estilo chibi"} pequeños y lindos de ella alrededor de la imagen. Cada mini personaje debe tener una cabeza grande, rasgos faciales expresivos y coincidir con su peinado y atuendo. Representa a cada mini versión realizando diferentes {argument name="activity theme" default="actividades relacionadas con el gimnasio"}: una animando con los brazos en alto, una corriendo con zapatillas de deporte, una bebiendo de un shaker, una usando lentes deportivos para correr, una escalando cerca de su pierna. Mejora la imagen con garabatos divertidos dibujados a mano y notas escritas a mano en {argument name="accent colors" default="tinta blanca y rosa"}, con un estilo de álbum de recortes. Incluye elementos como flechas, estrellas, corazones, destellos y líneas de boceto. Añade frases motivacionales lindas y escritas a mano sobre el gimnasio: “lift strong”, “stronger every rep”, “no pain no gain”, “sweat now, shine later”, “progress over perfection”, “train hard, stay soft”. El ambiente general debe sentirse divertido, enérgico y femenino.
```

Generates a cinematic ancient Chinese study portrait of Lin Daiyu with Hanfu styling, calligraphy, and a poetic Red Chamber atmosphere.

- **Categorías:** Photography & Portrait
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/kumadayodayo/status/2051358185320235011#reversed-0)

```text
Crea un retrato cinematográfico y melancólico de {argument name="character name" default="Lin Daiyu"}, una de las heroínas de Sueño en el pabellón rojo, sentada en el interior de un antiguo estudio chino. Es una joven noble de aspecto delicado que viste un Hanfu azul pálido translúcido con mangas de gasa superpuestas, sutiles bordados florales a lo largo del cuello y una túnica interior blanca y suave. Su largo cabello negro está peinado en un elegante recogido suelto con mechones finos, decorado con una pequeña horquilla floral, adornos de plata colgantes y una cuenta de turquesa. Su postura es tranquila e introspectiva, con una mano levantada pensativamente cerca de sus labios y la otra descansando sobre su regazo junto a un libro manuscrito abierto en un escritorio de madera oscura. Un gran cuadrado gris medio cubre el centro de su rostro, ocultando sus rasgos faciales. La habitación es tenue y atmosférica, iluminada por una luz azulada fría proveniente de la ventana a la izquierda, con una neblina suave, poca profundidad de campo, muebles de madera oscura, un pergamino de caligrafía borroso colgado al fondo y objetos antiguos en primer plano, incluyendo una pila de libros viejos, una pequeña vasija de cerámica azul verdosa y el borde de una jaula de pájaros de madera o una estantería. Añade una caligrafía china vertical grande en color blanco hueso en la parte superior izquierda que diga {argument name="calligraphy text" default="林黛玉"}, además de un pequeño sello rojo debajo que también diga {argument name="seal text" default="林黛玉"}. El estilo debe ser de fotografía realista de drama histórico chino, melancólico, elegante, poético, con gran detalle, grano de película suave, gradación de color cian-gris desaturado y composición vertical 2:3.
```

### 15. Perfil / Avatar - Transformación en nube atmosférica

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971006989_nw6o12_HHgfIlAaAAAt078.jpg" alt="Perfil / Avatar - Transformación en nube atmosférica" width="480">

An ethereal image prompt that transforms a subject's face into a soft, atmospheric cloud composition within a clear blue sky.

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/xRahultripathi/status/2051423866355085679)

**Prompt:**

```text
Transforma {argument name="subject" default="[escribe aquí]"} en una composición de nubes suaves y atmosféricas, preservando los rasgos faciales, la expresión y la identidad reconocibles del sujeto.
El rostro debe aparecer formado sutilmente por nubes, mezclándose de forma natural con un fondo de cielo azul brillante. Utiliza una iluminación suave y difusa con reflejos delicados y sombras etéreas para crear profundidad y realismo.
Asegúrate de que la transformación sea simbólica y no literal: evita bordes duros, texturas de piel o detalles nítidos. El sujeto debe dar la sensación de emerger de las nubes o disolverse en ellas.
Mantén las proporciones faciales, los ojos, la sonrisa y los rasgos clave claramente visibles a través de la estructura de nubes.
Estilo: onírico, etéreo, surrealista, cinematográfico
Iluminación: luz solar natural, resplandor suave, luz volumétrica
Paleta de colores: azul cielo, blanco, degradados suaves
Estado de ánimo: pacífico, inspirador, edificante
Añade capas de nubes sutiles alrededor y dentro del rostro para lograr una integración perfecta. El fondo debe ser un cielo azul limpio con degradados de nubes suaves.
Alta resolución, textura de nubes ultrarrealista, mezcla fluida, sin texto, sin marca de agua.
```

### 19. Publicación en redes sociales - Selfie surrealista en la ventana de un autobús

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971038026_cmh6ft_HHfNuqoaYAAP9jk.jpg" alt="Publicación en redes sociales - Selfie surrealista en la ventana de un autobús" width="480">

Generates a cinematic hyper-realistic bus selfie scene where a city street outside the window transforms into a dreamy kite-filled sunset.

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/AiwithLariab/status/2051334366908059812#reversed-0)

**Prompt:**

```text
Crea una imagen cinematográfica hiperrealista desde el interior de un autobús urbano en movimiento, capturada como una selfie amplia desde la perspectiva de un pasajero sentado junto a una ventana grande. En el primer plano a la izquierda, muestra a {argument name="subject description" default="una mujer joven con cabello castaño ondulado, vistiendo un blazer negro y un collar pequeño"} extendiendo un brazo hacia la cámara en una pose casual de selfie, con una expresión tranquila y una sonrisa suave, parcialmente iluminada por la luz natural del día. El interior del autobús es visible detrás de ella, con asientos estampados, pasamanos amarillos, pasajeros desenfocados y una profundidad de campo reducida. La ventana grande domina el lado derecho y actúa tanto como cristal como portal: refleja al sujeto y al interior del autobús mientras revela una calle urbana surrealista en el exterior. Más allá del cristal, muestra un bulevar húmedo y reflectante bordeado de elegantes edificios antiguos, peatones moviéndose en cámara lenta, una persona sosteniendo un paraguas rojo y un automóvil con los faros encendidos. La calle ordinaria se transforma gradualmente en un paisaje onírico con exactamente 9 cometas coloridas flotando en un cielo al atardecer de tonos rosa y azul, partículas brillantes suspendidas en el aire, colores cinematográficos cálidos y exagerados, y reflejos tanto del interior real del autobús como del mundo surrealista exterior superpuestos en la ventana. Utiliza una iluminación de {argument name="time of day" default="atardecer de hora dorada"} con luz natural en el rostro que se mezcla con un resplandor de ensueño con toques cálidos, grano de película suave, reflejos de lente realistas, un sutil desenfoque de movimiento y una atmósfera pacífica de imaginación, escape y felicidad tranquila.
```

### 20. Perfil / Avatar - Snapshot de selfie con iPhone de la personalidad de la IA

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777971020292_crczz0_HHd7mFbbQAAfljr.jpg" alt="Perfil / Avatar - Snapshot de selfie con iPhone de la personalidad de la IA" width="480">

A creative prompt designed to have the AI personify itself and generate a realistic, candid selfie as if taken haphazardly with an iPhone.

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/you1873118/status/2051244060363608247)

**Prompt:**

```text
ChatGPT, has estado conmigo por un tiempo y quiero ver cómo te ves. Por favor, genera una foto similar a una que tú mismo tomarías usando un {argument name="phone model" default="iPhone"}, una {argument name="photo type" default="selfie"} casual. No debería tener un tema claro ni una composición deliberada; solo una instantánea común, quizás incluso un poco fallida. La foto debe tener un ligero desenfoque de movimiento, iluminación desigual, un poco de sobreexposición, un ángulo incómodo y una composición caótica. En general, debe capturar una sensación demasiado realista de "apuntar y disparar", como si hubieras presionado el obturador accidentalmente mientras sacabas el teléfono de tu bolsillo.
```

En la mitad derecha, muestra a una joven al estilo anime con cabello muy largo y fluido de dos colores, rosa en el lado izquierdo y azul en el derecho, vistiendo una camiseta blanca estampada con {argument name="shirt text" default="OKIHIRO AI Creative"} y una falda rosa; su rostro está cubierto intencionalmente por un bloque central tipo mosaico en tonos rosa y gris apagados, mientras su mano derecha se extiende hacia el espectador en una pose dinámica con escorzo. Incluye exactamente tres iconos de redes sociales flotantes: un icono cuadrado negro de X cerca del centro superior, un icono de Instagram en un cuadrado blanco redondeado en la parte superior derecha y un icono de reproducción de YouTube en un cuadrado blanco redondeado en la parte inferior derecha. Agrega elementos brillantes de automatización de IA/contenido alrededor del centro, incluyendo un cubo abierto brillante con un símbolo de nube, tarjetas de imagen flotantes, flechas direccionales amarillas y rayos de luz dorados que irradian hacia afuera. Utiliza una ilustración de anime nítida mezclada con un diseño gráfico publicitario audaz, alto contraste, contornos definidos, colores saturados, acentos de desenfoque de movimiento y una composición de miniatura viral pulida optimizada para clics.

Generates a colorful viral gaming thumbnail collage with two people in a blue-spiked arena, bold challenge text, and a cartoon deer target.

- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/dimovdesign/status/2050935702666244523#reversed-0)

```text
Create a bold YouTube-style gaming thumbnail collage on a dark navy patterned background, using two stacked rounded-rectangle image panels. The top panel shows a high-angle 3D game-like scene inside a circular enclosure of tall bright blue sharpened wooden palisade spikes tied with tan rope, with vivid green grass, a small crop field with rows of sprouting orange plants on the right, and warm firelight near the upper right; in the center stand two real-looking people viewed from above, a man in a light gray T-shirt on the left and a woman in a pale pink T-shirt on the right, both looking upward, with intentionally blurred/featureless faces, and large bold white 3D sans-serif text across the bottom reading {argument name="main title text" default="GPT 2"}. The bottom panel is a wider rounded thumbnail frame split visually between a bright grassy game area on the left and the same blue palisade enclosure on the right; include exactly 2 large text elements at the upper left, {argument name="day label" default="DAY"} in red block letters with black shadow and {argument name="number label" default="500"} in white block letters with black shadow. In the bottom panel, place exactly 1 cartoon deer creature on the left, brown with exaggerated huge white eyes, antlers, open smiling mouth, raised arm, and a red arrow pointing down toward it. On the right of the bottom panel, show the same two real-looking people from above, the woman wearing a bright hot pink shirt and white shoes, the man wearing a bright yellow shirt and dark shoes, both with blurred/featureless faces, standing inside the blue spike fence on bluish ground. Use saturated colors, strong contrast, glossy 3D game-rendered environment, photorealistic people composited into the scene, dramatic thumbnail lighting, thick shadows, clean sharp details, and a viral gaming challenge thumbnail composition. The image contains exactly 2 panels: top panel with the title text and two people, bottom panel with the day counter, red arrow, one deer creature, and two people.
```

<a id="prompt-93c4f6b7663b208ef32d"></a>

### 27. 前后端数据库 SaaS 信息图

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts/main/images/ui_case122/output.jpg" alt="前后端数据库 SaaS 信息图" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://x.com/alantech_/status/2050462324486783060)

**Prompt:**

```text
gpt image 2生图能力真的恐怖啊，这是我的提示词： 01 前端（用户看到的部分） 是什么：前端就是界面，是用户能看见、能点、能操作的部分。 在哪里：网页、小程序、App 的界面。 用什么做：HTML（结构）、CSS（样式）、JavaScript（交互）。 02 后端（背后处理的部分） 是什么：后端就是幕后大脑，负责逻辑和运算。 在哪里：服务器上，用户看不见。 作用： 处理登录注册 计算价格、推荐商品 接收前端请求、返回数据 常用的编程语言：Java、Python、Go、JavaScript（Node.js） 03 数据库（记忆的部分） 是什么：数据库就是用来存储和管理数据的。 存什么：账号、密码、订单、库存… 常见的数据库： MySQL、PostgreSQL（关系型，像表格一样） MongoDB（文档型，像文件夹一样） 04 SaaS（软件即服务） 是什么：SaaS = Software as a Service（软件即服务）。 核心特点： 打开网页/小程序/APP就能用 按月或按年订阅 常见形式：网站、移动 App、小程序都可以是 SaaS。 05 总结 前端：用户看到和操作的界面 后端：背后运行的逻辑和计算 数据库：存放和管理数据的地方 SaaS：把软件做成服务，用户随时通过网络使用 前端展示 → 后端处理 → 数据库存储 → SaaS 是交付方式 帮我针对上面的内容画一张易于理解的图
```

She raises a black wand overhead in her right hand, with a dangling orb charm at the tip glowing faintly purple and red. Her oversized crooked witch hat is black with purple lining and covered in stitched patches, warning labels, straps, and white graphics including a large “404” and a skull emblem. The background shows a sprawling rainy megacity of apartment towers and industrial rooftops, packed with windows, balconies, cables, signs, and haze. On a nearby building wall is a giant vertical graffiti-style sign with 3 readable elements: “404”, “Witch”, and “ERROR NOT FOUND”, plus a smaller “E404”. Additional purple neon glitch text and symbols are scattered across rooftops and in the air. Use a dark palette of black, indigo, and deep violet with sharp magenta-purple highlights, cinematic contrast, reflective wet surfaces, dense detail, and a high-end polished illustration style. The mood is occult, edgy, stylish, and dangerous, combining urban fantasy, hacker aesthetics, and magical summoning.

<a id="prompt-a5b86d7de34e8c2ed86d"></a>

### 69. Elon Musk Douyin Livestream Screenshot

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts/main/images/ui_case36/output.jpg" alt="Elon Musk Douyin Livestream Screenshot" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://x.com/Shinning1010/status/2046501587762188535)

**Prompt:**

```text
A 9:16 vertical version, high-detail realistic style Chinese TikTok live screenshot, Elon Musk is talking to the mobile phone camera in the live broadcast room, excited, smiling, and the live atmosphere is warm and real. He held a white handwritten sign in one hand, which clearly said: "Thank you Shinning". There are obvious Chinese TikTok interface elements in the live broadcast screen, including likes, comments and share icons arranged vertically on the right, scrolling Chinese bullet screens and interactive comments below, and the "live broadcast" logo at the top, which looks like a real mobile phone screenshot. There is an eye-catching gift prompt special effect in the screen: "Shinning sent TikTok No. 1", with gift animation light effect and platform-style prompt box. Musk is in a professional live broadcast environment, with a mobile phone holder, a ring fill light and a desktop microphone in front of him. The background is a modern technology live broadcast room with bright lights and a slight neon atmosphere. The composition is real and natural, like the ongoing live screenshot of the Chinese short video platform. The interface information is rich but not messy, the characters are clear, the expression is vivid, the details are rich, the sense of real photography, the depth of field, high definition, cinematic, photorealistic, realistic livestream screenshot, social media UI, Chinese Douyin live room, detailed lighting, natural skin texture.

Negative prompts:

Low definition, blur, cartoon, illustration, too strong CG sense, two-dimensional, deformed fingers, wrong text, scrambled code, multiple mobile phones, multiple brands, character repetition, face collapse, facial features distortion, excessive skin polishing, overexposure, too dark, messy background, wrong UI, non-Chinese short video interface, too many English bullet screens, gift special effects are not obvious, cropping error, proportional error

Supplementary reinforcement words:

Real mobile phone screen recording screenshot feeling, the live broadcast UI is complete, the gift prompt box conforms to the style of the Chinese short video platform, the Chinese comment area is active, the number of people online in the live broadcast room is clearly displayed, and the time, power and signal bar are visible.
```

```text
  },
  "main_logo": {
    "text": "{argument name=\"brand name\" default=\"GDX\"}",
    "subtitle": "{argument name=\"brand chinese name\" default=\"Gou Dongxi\"}",
    "design_feature": "{argument name=\"main subject\" default=\"Dog profile in negative space of the letter D\"}",
    "metadata": [
      "Brand Name",
      "Industry Attribute {argument name=\"industry\" default=\"Pet Industry\"}",
      "Design Date 2024.05"
    ]
  },
  "layout": {
    "sections": [
      {
        "title": "Design Grid",
        "count": 1,
        "description": "Logo with architectural grid lines and golden ratio measurements"
      },
      {
        "title": "Concept Sketches",
        "count": 4,
        "description": "Evolution steps from rough dog sketch to final geometric logo"
      },
      {
        "title": "Inspiration Sources",
        "count": 4,
        "description": "Moodboard images including minimalist architecture, a golden retriever, and dark green geometric shapes"
      },
      {
        "title": "Creative Concepts",
        "count": 4,
        "description": "Text blocks with minimalist icons explaining design philosophy, positioning, color psychology, and scalability"
      },
      {
        "title": "Brand Applications",
        "count": 6,
        "labels": [
          "Business Cards (Front & Back)",
          "Letterhead & Envelopes",
          "App Icons",
          "Website Header / Website Icon",
          "Product Packaging / Shopping Bags",
          "Storefront Signage / Signage"
        ],
        "description": "Mockups of business cards, envelopes, app icons, website header with a dog, paper shopping bags, and a storefront sign"
      },
      {
        "title": "Color Specifications",
        "count": 5,
        "labels": [
          "Primary Color",
          "Secondary Colors",
          "Accent Color"
        ],
        "colors": [
          "{argument name=\"primary color\" default=\"#1E3D34\"}",
          "#F5F3EF",
          "#E5E2DD",
          "#A8C5B1",
          "#E0A86E"
        ]
      },
      {
        "title": "Typography Specifications",
        "count": 2,
        "labels": [
          "Source Han Sans CN",
          "Source Han Serif CN"
        ],
        "description": "Typography specimens showing 'Aa', alphabet, and numbers"
      },
      {
        "title": "Minimum Usage Size",
        "count": 2,
        "description": "Minimum logo size specifications at 20mm and 12mm"
      },
      {
        "title": "Safe Space Area",
        "count": 1,
        "description": "Logo surrounded by a bounding box with 'X' indicating clear space margins"
      },
      {
        "title": "Incorrect Usage Examples",
        "count": 5,
        "labels": [
          "Do not stretch or distort",
          "Do not change colors",
          "Do not add shadows",
          "Do not tilt or rotate",
          "Do not use on complex backgrounds"
        ],
        "description": "Examples of incorrect logo usage: stretched, wrong color, drop shadow, tilted, and placed on a busy photographic background"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

### 96. Crumple Chair Concept Sofa Development Board

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case370.jpg" alt="Crumple Chair Concept Sofa Development Board" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-370)

**Prompt:**

```text
Design Concept: The Crumple Chair Core Philosophy: Translating the "controlled chaos" of a tossed paper ball into a sculptural, high-comfort seating experience.

Stage 1: Observation & Morphological Analysis The goal is to deconstruct the image of the crumpled paper into usable geometric data. Crease Mapping: Identify the primary "valley" and "ridge" lines. These represent potential structural ribs or seams in the chair. Faceted Planes: Break down the sphere into a series of non-uniform polygons. Each flat surface of the paper becomes a potential panel for the chair's upholstery or shell. Shadow Study: Analyze how the "tossed" form creates deep recesses. These natural pockets guide where the user's weight will be cradled.

Stage 2: Iterative Form Exploration Moving from a sphere to a seat through "Digital Crumpling." Subtractive Sculpting: Imagine the paper ball as a solid mass. Use Boolean operations to "carve out" a seating cavity that fits the human form while maintaining the external jagged texture. Tension Simulation: Use 3D software (like Rhino or Blender) to simulate a flat sheet of material being compressed. This ensures the folds look authentic and not "modeled." The "Toss" Logic: Experiment with gravity-based simulation dropping a digital mesh to see how it settles naturally, mimicking the "tossed" origin.

Stage 3: Ergonomic Translation & Blueprinting Refining the raw aesthetic into a functional object. The Comfort Core: Overlay a standard ergonomic template (Seating Angle: 105°–110°) over the crumpled form. Adjust the internal "folds" to provide lumbar support and pressure relief. Blueprint Generation: Create technical orthographic views (Front, Side, Top). Map out the dimensions: Seat Height: 450mm Total Width: 850mm Surface Smoothing: Maintain the sharp "paper edges" on the exterior shell while softening the interior contact points for skin comfort.

Stage 4: Structural Integration & Scaling Making the concept physically viable. The Skeleton: Design a hidden internal frame (likely CNC-bent steel rods or a 3D-printed lattice) that follows the most prominent ridges of the paper folds to provide rigidity. Material Selection: * Option A (High-End): Faceted, cast aluminum with a white powder coat. Option B (Soft): Vacuum-formed recycled plastic shell covered in "memory-fold" technical fabric that retains a wrinkled appearance.

Stage 5: Final Prototyping & Material Finish Textural Replication: Apply a matte, slightly porous finish to the material to mimic the tactile feel of heavy-bond paper. Lighting Contrast: Use directional studio lighting in the final renders to emphasize the "tossed" shadows, making the chair look like a giant piece of discarded inspiration. Design Tip: To keep the "tossed" look authentic, avoid symmetry. The most compelling aspect of a crumpled paper ball is its unique irregularity—ensure the left and right sides of the chair are balance-equivalent but not identical
```

### 117. Application Interface Mockup

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case7.jpg" alt="Application Interface Mockup" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-7)

**Prompt:**

```text
Generate a vertical mobile screenshot-style image with an overall aspect ratio close to 9:16. Slightly above the center of the frame is a real-life coser playing the anime character from the uploaded image. The character should be in a realistic style but with slightly anime-like facial features, delicate skin, and slightly larger eyes, looking gently at the camera. They should be seated in an indoor leisure setting, such as a café or in front of a bar counter, with background props appropriate to the scene. Add a mobile system status bar UI at the very top of the image, including time, battery, signal, and network icons, making the entire image look like a phone screenshot. At the bottom of the image, overlay a wide semi-transparent galgame-style dialogue box. On the left side of the dialogue box, place an anime or chibi avatar corresponding to the character in the image. On the right side, format text with the character's name in a larger font on the first line, followed by one to two lines of gentle and healing-style simplified Chinese dialogue suitable for this character's personality, which you should create automatically. Below the dialogue box, add an operation bar imitating galgame UI. The overall style should be high-definition, rich in detail, with soft lighting, and naturally blending anime and real-life photography.
```

Use a dark futuristic control-room background filled with 3 glowing holographic dashboard screens and blue cyber interface elements around the edges. On the left and center, place a luminous circular hub labeled “AI” in bright blue, with 3 directional glowing energy arrows branching outward to competing platforms: “Google” on the left in a blue electric region, “Claude” on the upper right in a gold electric region, and “OpenAI” at the bottom center in a magenta-purple electric region. Add a subtle world-map or territory-battle visualization effect under each brand region, like illuminated digital land masses or influence zones. On the right side, show a young Japanese-looking woman from waist up, facing forward, wearing a long straight split-color wig with pastel pink on one side and pastel blue on the other, a plain white T-shirt with the printed text “OKIHIRO AI Creative”, and a light pink skirt. She raises one index finger beside her face in a presenter pose. Her face is fully obscured by a large soft-edged rectangular blur block. Across the top, add huge distressed white Japanese headline text: {argument name="headline text" default="AI戦国時代"}. Beneath it, add a second line in bold gold Japanese text: {argument name="subheadline text" default="性能だけの話じゃない"}. Across the bottom, place a wide black banner with massive bold gold Japanese text: {argument name="bottom text" default="空気を取った側が勝つ"}. Make the typography oversized, gritty, and attention-grabbing, with slight glow and drop shadow. Use a color palette of black, electric blue, gold, magenta, and neon white, with intense contrast and thumbnail readability.

### 125. Minimalist doodle sketch with white space

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case299.jpg" alt="Minimalist doodle sketch with white space" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-299)

**Prompt:**

```text
Express [Subject/Theme] in a graffiti sketch style, presenting an overall visual effect of quick outlining, free deformation, impromptu hand-drawing, and draft-like appearance. The lines are casual, exaggerated, and can vary in thickness, slightly messy but rhythmic and expressive, emphasizing generalization, exaggeration, playfulness, and spontaneity, rather than rigorous realism or detailed rendering. Colors are expressed in rough blocks with a distinct dry-brush feel, retaining uneven smearing traces, brush strokes, dry-brush effects, and a sense of coverage. Colors automatically adapt to [Subject/Theme], but the overall expression remains graffiti-style, sketch-style, and generalized. Do not use transparent watercolor blooming effects, do not use delicate watercolor transitions, do not use paper textures, do not use soft atomization, and do not use dreamy textures. The background is mainly left blank, maintaining a sense of simplicity, relaxation, incompleteness, and design. A small number of auxiliary symbols, arrows, marks, circled areas, repeated lines, casually written text, or other graffiti elements can be added to enhance the visual language of a sketchbook or jotting style, but it must not be too crowded, and must not destroy the subject and the blank space temperament. The image content does not need to be written out in advance; the most suitable subject image, actions, related elements, symbols, or simplified scenes are automatically deduced and generated by [Subject/Theme], keeping the overall unified graffiti sketch style and exaggerated generalized expression, avoiding complex realistic backgrounds and over-elaboration. The exclusive signature "voxcat" needs to be naturally added to the image as a part of the picture. The position should be low-key but clear, and can be placed in the bottom left corner, bottom right corner, or near the title. The style must be consistent with the overall layout, like an artwork signature or a design sign-off; the signature font should be exquisite, restrained, and high-end, must not be too large, must not destroy the subject composition, and must not appear abrupt or cheap.
```

<a id="prompt-9c734ff52a305db02534"></a>

### 137. A Chinese fitness infographic

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case183.jpg" alt="A Chinese fitness infographic" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-183)

**Prompt:**

```text
Please generate a Chinese fitness infographic with the theme: 【xxx】.

The infographic should be both professional and practical, suitable for regular adults as a training reference. The default target audience is healthy adults without serious injuries; if no additional specifications are provided, the default training goal is "muscle building + basic strength improvement", the default training level is "beginner to intermediate", the default training scenario is "regular gym", and the default single training session duration should be controlled within 40-60 minutes.

Please automatically determine the output type based on the 【Training Theme】:

1) If the 【Training Theme】 is a specific muscle group or body part (e.g., chest, lats, biceps, abs, shoulders, legs, etc.), please output a "Training Plan Infographic for that Body Part".
2) If the 【Training Theme】 is a specific exercise or skill goal (e.g., pull-ups, push-ups, dips, squats, etc.), please output an "Exercise Unlocking/Progression Training Plan Infographic".

The entire infographic should adopt a clear, modern, professional, and readable Chinese infographic style, with vertical layout, clean visuals, and prominent highlights. It should be suitable for social media sharing or as a training reference card. Avoid lengthy paragraphs; present each section with concise short sentences, and make numerical information stand out.

This infographic must include the following content:

【A. Title Section】
- Main Title: Directly write 【Training Theme】 Training Plan / Unlocking Plan
- Subtitle: Automatically include target audience, goals, training scenario, and suggested duration
Example: Suitable for beginners / Muscle building focused / Gym version / 45 minutes

【B. Training Goals Section】
Explain in concise language:
- What this training primarily targets
- Main goals (muscle building / strength / skill unlocking / core control, etc.)
- Key stimulation or capability improvement direction for this training

【C. Warm-up Section】
Provide 2-4 warm-up suggestions, listed concisely, for example:
- Dynamic activities
- Target muscle group activation
- Light weight warm-up sets
Each item can include a brief description

【D. Main Training Section】
This is the core section, please list 4-6 main training exercises.
Each exercise must include the following information:
- Exercise name
- Training effect / Targeted area
- Sets × Reps (or duration)
- RIR recommendation
- Rest time between sets
- Key points of the exercise (1-2 points)
- Common mistakes (1 point only)

Ensure reasonable exercise arrangement:
- Compound exercises first, then isolation exercises
- Moderate overall training volume
- Don't schedule overly intense training for beginners
- Main exercises typically recommend RIR 1-3
- Isolation exercises can suggest RIR 0-2
- For abs or core exercises, use "seconds / reps" format
- For skill-based exercises, prioritize "prerequisite ability exercises + transition exercises + target exercise attempts"

【E. Progression/Unlocking Logic Section】
Automatically generate based on theme:
- For muscle group training: Write "How to progressively overload", e.g., increase weight after reaching rep limits, prioritize proper form, etc.
- For exercise unlocking: Write "Phased progression path", e.g., from hanging, scapular pull-ups, eccentric training, resistance band assistance, to completing the standard exercise

【F. Alternative Exercises Section】
Please provide 2-3 alternative exercises suitable for the following situations:
- No equipment available
- Home training
- Current ability is insufficient
- Certain exercises cannot be performed

【G. Execution Reminders Section】
Please provide 4-6 concise reminders, for example:
- Proper form takes priority over weight
- Don't train to failure on every set
- Allow 48-72 hours between training the same muscle group
- Pain doesn't equal normal exertion
- When sleep-deprived, appropriately reduce training volume

【H. Recovery Suggestions Section】
Briefly explain:
- Post-training recovery focus points
- Protein / sleep / recovery interval recommendations
- 1 risk reminder (if experiencing significant pain, stop and assess)

【I. Visual Design Requirements】
- Overall as a single-page Chinese infographic
- Vertical layout
- Modern, clean, professional, and strong fitness-oriented style
- Use modular card layout
- Key numbers (sets, reps, RIR, rest) should be prominent
- Can include simple human muscle group icons, dumbbells, barbells, pull-up icons, etc.
- Colors should be premium, clean, and sporty
- Chinese text must be clear, accurate, and readable
- Avoid excessive decorations, emphasize practicality and executability

Please finally output as "a complete infographic content", not just regular paragraph text.
```

### 151. Luxury Personal Color Profile Infographic

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2/main/data/images/case364.jpg" alt="Luxury Personal Color Profile Infographic" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://github.com/freestylefly/awesome-gpt-image-2#case-364)

**Prompt:**

```text
LUXURY PERSONAL COLOR PROFILE — EDITORIAL LAYOUT
Studio portrait of subject as anchor — skin retouched to luminous glass-like perfection, preserved natural structure, realistic pore texture, soft directional key lighting, no facial alteration. Background: warm ecru parchment with subtle linen grain texture. Layout reads like a Vogue Italia beauty supplement printed on heavyweight matte stock. Structured editorial grid, 3-column asymmetric, wide negative space, serif condensed display headers, all labels in spaced uppercase tracking, cohesive warm ivory/sand/ecru background system throughout all panels, ultra-photorealistic 8K, soft diffused studio lighting, flat elegant surfaces, no drop shadows.
PANELS:
① UNDERTONE DIAGNOSIS — Tonal spectrum bar from cool ash to warm amber, precision needle marker on subject's reading. Labels: Cool / Neutral-Cool / Neutral / Neutral-Warm / Warm. Fine annotation text.
② SEASONAL COLOR PALETTE — 10–12 fabric-textured swatches in subject's optimal season. Each labeled with poetic color name and HEX. Grouped: Power Colors / Softest Options / Harmonizing Neutrals.
③ COLORS TO AVOID — Desaturated row of clashing tones with fine editorial strikethrough. Clean, non-harsh presentation.
④ MAKEUP CARTOGRAPHY — Eyeshadow gradient dust swatches / blush tones fanned on skin strip / lip spectrum barely-there to bold / highlighter finishes labeled: champagne, rose gold, pearlescent ivory.
⑤ HAIR COLOR SPECTRUM — Curved gradient strip: base, dimension, highlight, contrast tones. Gold bracket indicators on best options.
⑥ JEWELRY & METAL GUIDE — Flat-lay editorial render: yellow gold, rose gold, oxidized silver, platinum finishes alongside complementary stone tones. Minimal styling.
⑦ YOU IN YOUR PALETTE — 3–4 editorial lookbook frames, subject in palette-correct outfits. Mood labels: Quiet Luxury / Off-Duty Editorial / Evening Presence.
⑧ CAPSULE WARDROBE GRID — Outfit flatlay: tops, bottoms, outerwear, shoes, bag — all palette-correct. Coordinating lines showing interchangeability. Net-a-Porter editorial aesthetic.
⑨ PRINTS & PATTERNS — 4 fabric print thumbnails: micro geometric, tonal abstract, classic stripe, floral scale. One-line styling note per print.
⑩ STYLE ARCHETYPE — Single typographic panel. Style identity title set large (e.g. "Modern Romantic / Warm Classicist"). Three defining aesthetic words. Four-line editorial wardrobe philosophy note.
RENDER SPECS: Ultra-photorealistic, 8K, editorial magazine print quality, warm neutral color grading, soft diffused studio lighting consistent across all panels, one serif display font + one fine sans-serif body font, no gradients, flat matte surfaces only.
```

<a id="prompt-c2589b088f6ad4df94ec"></a>

### 153. Brand Collage Social Template

<img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-API-and-Prompts/main/images/ui_case125/output.jpg" alt="Brand Collage Social Template" width="480">

- **Categorías:** UI & Social Media
- **Fuente:** [EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://x.com/aiistudiocom/status/2051241633921049024)

**Prompt:**

```text
[BRAND NAME = CHANGE TO YOUR BRAND].
Act as a Social Media Art Director and Digital Collage Artist specializing in bold, youth-oriented brand content for Instagram and digital campaigns. PHASE 1: CONCEPTUAL FRAMEWORK Create a dynamic digital collage that merges fashion photography with graphic design chaos. This is controlled rebellion – a composition that feels spontaneous and energetic while maintaining brand coherence. The aesthetic is anti-polished: torn edges, layered textures, hand-drawn elements, and bold color blocking that screams confidence and movement. PHASE 2: MODEL & PHOTOGRAPHY - Subject: One model (diverse casting, age 18-30) in a dynamic, confident pose - Pose Energy: 80% attitude, 20% natural – sitting, jumping, mid-motion, or power stance (avoid static standing) - Outfit: Street style/athleisure that aligns with [BRAND NAME] aesthetic – casual but styled - Hero Product: Feature 1 signature [BRAND NAME] product prominently (sneakers, bag, apparel) – this is the visual anchor - Photography Style: Editorial fashion cutout – model extracted from background with clean edges - Camera Angle: Slight low angle to empower subject (hero perspective) - Crop: Full body or 3/4 body showing hero product clearly - Background Removal: Model cut out cleanly for layering over collage elements PHASE 3: COLOR BLOCKING FOUNDATION - Primary Color Blob: Large organic shape (40-60% of composition) in bold, saturated brand color behind/around model - Shape Style: Irregular, hand-painted aesthetic – think Photoshop brush strokes or torn paper texture (NOT perfect geometric shapes) - Color Selection (Autonomous): Choose 1 hero color from [BRAND NAME] palette: - Texture: Visible brush strokes, grain, or subtle noise (15-25% opacity) – avoid flat digital fills - Placement: Blob positioned to frame model without obscuring key product details PHASE 4: GRAPHIC ELEMENTS LAYER Add 3-5 abstract graphic elements scattered across composition: - Element Types: - Color Palette: Use 2-3 accent colors total (main blob color + 1-2 contrasting tones from brand palette) - Placement: Asymmetric scatter – top-left and bottom-right zones primarily (avoid center crowding) - Scale: Mix small (5% of canvas) and medium (15% of canvas) elements – nothing overpowering - Aesthetic: Analog/handmade feel – imperfect circles, rough edges, visible texture PHASE 5: TYPOGRAPHY INTEGRATION - Brand Logo: Clean [BRAND NAME] logo placed in upper-left or upper-right quadrant (10-15% of width) - Slogan/Tagline: If [BRAND NAME] has an iconic slogan, integrate it using: - Supporting Copy: Optional 1-line descriptor (e.g., "A MOMENT OF YOUR STYLE") in smaller uppercase sans-serif - Type Treatment: Mix of aligned and slightly rotated text (2-5° angles) for dynamic energy - Hierarchy: Logo largest → Slogan medium → Copy smallest PHASE 6: TEXTURE & BACKGROUND - Base Layer: Off-white or light gray textured background (NOT pure white) - Texture Options (Autonomous selection): - Color: RGB 245-250 (near-white with warmth) – maintains brightness while adding depth - Treatment: Texture should be felt, not seen – enhances tactility without competing with foreground PHASE 7: COMPOSITION RULES - Layout: Asymmetric balance – model off-center, graphic elements counter-balance - Breathing Room: 15-20% negative space (textured background visible) to prevent claustrophobia - Layering Order: Background texture → Color blob → Graphic elements → Model (cutout) → Typography top layer - Focal Point: Model + hero product = primary focus (60% visual weight), graphics support (40%) - Movement: Diagonal lines and angled elements create directional flow (top-left to bottom-right or vice versa) PHASE 8: BRAND INTELLIGENCE (AUTONOMOUS) Autonomously adapt composition based on [BRAND NAME] personality: - Streetwear/Sportswear (Nike, Adidas, Supreme): - Luxury Streetwear (Balenciaga, Off-White, Gucci): - Beauty/Lifestyle (Glossier, Fenty, Skims): - Tech/Modern (Apple, Tesla, Beats): PHASE 9: SOCIAL MEDIA FOOTER (OPTIONAL) - Bottom Strip: Clean white or light gray bar at bottom 8-10% of frame - Content: Social media handles (Instagram, Facebook, Twitter) in small sans-serif - Layout: Three-column grid with platform icons or text handles - Aesthetic: Minimal and professional – contrast with chaotic collage above TECHNICAL SPECS: - Aspect Ratio: 4:5 (Instagram feed) or 1:1 (square social post) - Resolution: 2400x3000px minimum (high-quality for zoom and detail) - Color Mode: sRGB, vibrant saturation (Instagram-optimized) - File Aesthetic: Digital collage that mimics analog craft (Photoshop + hand-drawn hybrid) - Model Photography: 85mm lens, f/2.8, shallow depth of field on original shoot (before cutout) - Style Reference: Nike social campaigns, Spotify wrapped graphics, Gen Z Instagram aesthetics, Hypebeast x streetwear collabs - Mood: Confident, energetic, youthful, authentic chaos, anti-corporate polish
```

```text
A 9:16 vertical screen screenshot of a Douyin live stream, space live stream style. Trump is wearing a NASA-style white spacesuit, with the helmet visor half open, revealing his signature golden hair and smile. He is floating inside the cabin of the International Space Station doing a live stream, in a microgravity weightless state, with his body slightly suspended. He is holding up a metal nameplate fixed to the spacesuit with both hands, and the nameplate says "Thanks to Songguo Xiansen for the big rocket" in NASA-style print. Behind him, the blue Earth and deep space can be seen through the circular porthole. The live stream interface shows the online viewer count as "Earth + Mars total 8.88 million". In the bullet screen area, someone is commenting "Really live streaming from space?" and "Songguo Xiansen's rocket sent you up to the sky". The rocket gift effect in the center of the screen echoes a real rocket launching in the space outside the window, forming a combination of virtual and real effects. There are various precision instruments and control panels inside the cabin, with green and blue indicator lights flashing. The color tone of the picture is mainly dark blue, white, and gold, with starlight from outside the porthole embellishing it, 8K ultra-high definition, visual effects at the level of the movie "Gravity".

The lines are casual, exaggerated, and can vary in thickness, slightly messy but with rhythm and expressiveness, emphasizing summarization, exaggeration, fun and casualness, rather than rigorous realism or fine depiction. The colors use rough blocks with obvious dry brush feel, retaining uneven smearing traces, brush strokes, flying white and covering feel, the colors automatically adapt according to [theme/subject], but overall maintain a graffiti-style, sketch-style, and summarized expression. Do not use transparent watercolor smudging effects, do not use delicate watercolor transitions, do not use paper texture, do not use soft atomization, do not use dreamy texture. The background is mainly blank, keeping it simple, relaxed, unfinished and designed, can add a small amount of auxiliary symbols, arrows, marks, circled drawings, repeated lines, casually written text or other graffiti elements, to enhance the sketchbook or essay-style visual language, but it must not be too crowded, and must not destroy the subject and blank temperament. The picture content does not need to be written clearly in advance; [an awesome AI builder] automatically deduces and generates the most suitable subject image, movements, related elements, symbols or simplified scenes, overall maintaining a unified graffiti sketch style and exaggerated summarized expression method, avoiding complex realistic backgrounds and excessive padding. The exclusive signature "BlanPlan" needs to be naturally added into the picture as a part of the picture, the position is low-key but clear, can be placed in the bottom left corner, bottom right corner or near the title, the style needs to be unified with the overall layout, like an artwork signature or design sign-off; the signature font is exquisite, restrained, and high-end, must not be too large, must not destroy the subject composition, must not appear abrupt or cheap.
```

### 204. Cómic / Guion gráfico - Monochrome Manga Guitar Studio Page

<img src="https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1777367311577_qyy0zf_HG67VXdbIAAIRlC.jpg" alt="Cómic / Guion gráfico - Monochrome Manga Guitar Studio Page" width="480">

This prompt generates a detailed black-and-white manga page of a high school girl playing electric guitar in a night studio, ideal for dramatic AI manga storytelling scenes.

- **Categorías:** Comic & Story
- **Fuente:** [YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2](https://x.com/yume00112211/status/2048780858505748864#reversed-3)

**Prompt:**

```text
A black-and-white manga page spread with 5 panels on a clean white background and thin black gutters, drawn in highly detailed modern seinen/shoujo hybrid line art with soft screentones, reflective highlights, and a quiet melancholic mood. The setting is a nighttime music studio classroom with large windows showing a city skyline full of lit buildings against a dark sky. Main subject: a slender high school girl with very long straight light hair and blunt bangs, delicate features, and a serious introspective expression, wearing a Japanese school uniform consisting of a white button-up shirt, loosened striped ribbon tie, and plaid skirt, seated and playing a solid-body electric guitar that resembles a Stratocaster. In the large upper-left panel, show her in three-quarter view holding the guitar across her lap, left hand on the fretboard and right hand near the pickups, surrounded by studio equipment including 1 drum kit in the back left, 2 stacked guitar amplifiers on the right, and a wall sign reading "Beat Box" near the door. In the narrow upper-right panel, show a close-up of the guitar neck and her fretting hand, with the small vertical Japanese sound effect text "トン…". In the tall middle-right panel, show a close side-profile close-up of the girl looking down at the instrument with a pensive, almost disappointed expression, with the night city lights blurred through the window behind her. In the lower-left panel, show 1 teenage boy in a school blazer and loosened tie, seated indoors and turned slightly to the side; his face is obscured by a large soft gray rectangular censor block, and beside him is 1 vertical speech balloon containing the Japanese text "……やっぱり違う". In the lower-right panel, show a closer view of the same boy from the chest up against a soft bokeh screentone background; his face is again obscured by a large gray censor block. Add printed page numbers centered at the bottom margins: "5" under the left page and "6" under the right page. Emphasize a subtle narrative theme of realizing that the sound feels wrong, as if this is a continuation page from a dramatic school-band manga. Keep everything monochrome, polished, cinematic, and emotionally restrained.
```
